## Surprising Connections (you probably didn't know these)
- `setupPlugin()` --indirect_call--> `scopeOf()`  [INFERRED]
  client/ui-approval/tests/ui-approval.client.spec.tsx → api/session-controller/src/client/scope.ts
- `afterPrompt()` --indirect_call--> `turns()`  [INFERRED]
  bundle/headless/tests/headless.spec.ts → experimental/claude-code-mods/src/types.ts
- `apply()` --indirect_call--> `loaded()`  [INFERRED]
  preset/agent-preset-registry/tests/mount.spec.ts → extensions/tool-cordis/tests/config.spec.ts
- `oneToolCall()` --calls--> `ToolCallId`  [EXTRACTED]
  acp/acp/tests/bridge.spec.ts → llm/llm/src/brand.ts
- `toolCallResponse()` --calls--> `ToolCallId`  [EXTRACTED]
  acp/acp/tests/edges.spec.ts → llm/llm/src/brand.ts

## Import Cycles
- 2-file cycle: `experimental/claude-code-mods/src/elements.ts -> experimental/claude-code-mods/src/types.ts -> experimental/claude-code-mods/src/elements.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `api/session-controller/src/client/index.ts -> api/session-controller/src/client/sessions/service.ts -> api/session-controller/src/client/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `boot/app-boot/src/index.ts -> boot/app-boot/src/profile-context.ts -> boot/app-boot/src/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `boot/app-boot/src/index.ts -> boot/app-boot/src/profile.ts -> boot/app-boot/src/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `sandbox/sandbox/src/index.ts -> sandbox/sandbox/src/roots.ts -> sandbox/sandbox/src/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `core/session/src/index.ts -> core/session/src/preparation.ts -> core/session/src/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `util/values/src/index.ts -> util/values/src/partial-json.ts -> util/values/src/index.ts`
- 2-file cycle: `core/scope/src/index.ts -> core/scope/src/store.ts -> core/scope/src/index.ts`
- 3-file cycle: `client/ui-settings-models/src/client/DeepSeekModelsEditor.tsx -> client/ui-settings-models/src/client/ModelRow.tsx -> client/ui-settings-models/src/client/ModelInputTypes.tsx -> client/ui-settings-models/src/client/DeepSeekModelsEditor.tsx`
